# Current Session Index - PERFORMANCE-OPTIMIZATION-TENANCY-REALTIME-20261031

- **Root causes confirmed & verified**:
  1. Tenancy access: `loadCompanyAccess` previously issued up to 3 separate sequential queries to `companies` (archive check, selected company, and `loadWorkspaceChoices`), causing extra round trips.
  2. Branch realtime: `BranchInboxRealtime` triggered `router.refresh()` on every SSE `ready` and `lost`, inducing double page re-renders on periodic 45s connection rotation.
  3. Database indexes review: The 3 proposed candidate indexes were evaluated against existing schema:
     - `company_members`: already indexed on `(user_id, status, company_id)` via `company_members_user_status_idx`.
     - `profiles(user_id)`: `user_id` is already PRIMARY KEY with unique index (`profiles_pkey`). Adding an index would duplicate the primary key.
     - `companies`: Primary key on `id` is used for point lookups; filtering `archived_at` in application code is fast. A partial index is unjustified and no new production SQL is applied.
- **Implemented changes**:
  1. `src/lib/tenancy/access.ts`: Cached company display names during archive validation in `loadCompanyAccess`, eliminating the duplicate `loadWorkspaceChoices` query. Retained strict session checks, revocation validation, generation cookie binding, and kept `company_members` out of `loadAuth` to protect `requireVerifiedUser` and admin routes.
  2. `src/components/app/branch-inbox-realtime.tsx`: Removed `router.refresh()` on normal SSE disconnect/rotation; added `onInboxEvent` for delta updates; preserved 250ms debouncing, reconnect/visibility catch-up, and revocation cleanup.
- **Verification**:
  - 8 test files / 96 tests PASS (`access-recovery.test.ts`, `access-contracts.test.ts`, `branch-inbox-realtime.test.tsx`, `branch-inbox-events.test.ts`, `inbox-events/route.test.ts`, `conversations-inbox.test.ts`, `inbox-failure-presentation.test.tsx`, `unified-inbox-safety.test.tsx`).
  - Full TypeScript `tsc --noEmit` PASS (0 errors).
  - Scoped ESLint PASS (0 errors).
  - No new production SQL or background runner added; WhatsApp and AI provider calls untouched.

# Current Session Index - SETUP-NAVIGATION-AND-NEXT-STEP-20261030

- **Task (Radical Fix for `/setup` Navigation & Next Step Logic — SINGLE-SETUP-PATH-001):**
  - **Defect Identified & Addressed:** In `paid-setup-workspace.tsx`, `nextStep` was previously searched across all steps starting from index 0 (`whatsapp`), which caused the "Next step" button to display `Next step: Connect WhatsApp` on every screen (including `hours`, `knowledge`, `language`, etc.) and jump backwards to WhatsApp.
  - **Core Fixes Implemented (`src/components/app/paid-setup-workspace.tsx`, `src/components/app/paid-assistant-checklist.tsx`, `src/components/app/paid-setup-workspace.module.css`):**
    - **Relative Sequence Calculation:** Replaced global search with linear forward progression relative to `activeStepId`: `nextCandidate = steps[position + 1]`.
    - **Dependency & State Bounding:** Evaluated `nextAllowed = workspaceStepAllowed(read, scope, nextCandidate.id, availableStepIds)`. If prerequisites are unmet, the button renders in a disabled state with `aria-disabled="true"` and an explicit Arabic/English explanation badge (`stepBlockedReason`) such as `بانتظار: مواعيد العمل` or `بانتظار التحقق من ربط واتساب`, preventing dependency bypass.
    - **Symmetric Navigation Controls:** Kept backward navigation strictly via header `previousStep` and checklist rail buttons; forward button only moves to `nextCandidate`. On the final step (`launch`), no forward button is rendered.
    - **Automatic Forward Transition on Save:** In `nextWorkspaceSelection`, removed the backwards fallback (`?? nextIncompleteOpen(0)`). Once a step (e.g. `hours`) is saved and confirmed by server snapshot, `nextWorkspaceSelection` automatically advances forward to the newly-unlocked step (`knowledge`).
    - **Selection & Saved Progress Recovery on Refresh:** In `PaidSetupBranchRead`, hydrated `selected` step from tenant/branch-scoped `sessionStorage` (`velora:setup:step:${companyId}:${branchId}`). Validated against server snapshot on arrival: respects active selection if allowed, advances if verified, and falls back to first incomplete open step from server progress if clean or invalidated.
    - **WhatsApp Isolation Maintained:** Waiting on commercial WhatsApp does not block business setup steps (`hours` -> `knowledge` -> `language` -> `test` -> `team`), while `launch` remains strictly gated on all prerequisites including commercial WhatsApp verification.
  - **Verification:**
    - Targeted Vitest: 22 test files, 335/335 PASS (added 4 new tests in `paid-setup-workspace.interaction.test.tsx` and 2 in `paid-setup-workspace.test.tsx`).

# Current Session Index - I02-3-FIX-RECEIVE-ONLY-RPC-SCHEMA-20261029

- **Task `I02.3` — Root Cause Fix for `public.save_whatsapp_inbound_receive_only`:**
  - **Issue Identified & Confirmed:** Production schema inspection of `public.whatsapp_inbound_jobs` verified that columns `next_retry_at` and `last_error_code` do not exist on the table. The table schema contains: `next_attempt_at` (`NOT NULL DEFAULT now()`), `status`, `claim_token`, `claim_expires_at`, `completed_at`, `result_code`, `attempt_count`, `created_at`, `updated_at`, etc. Attempting to update non-existent columns caused PostgreSQL execution failures.
  - **Remediation Migration (`supabase/migrations/20261029224500_fix_save_whatsapp_inbound_receive_only_schema.sql`):**
    - Corrected the `UPDATE public.whatsapp_inbound_jobs` statement to:
      ```sql
      update public.whatsapp_inbound_jobs
         set status = 'completed',
             claim_token = null,
             claim_expires_at = null,
             completed_at = coalesce(completed_at, pg_catalog.clock_timestamp()),
             result_code = 'SETUP_META_TEST_RECEIVE_ONLY',
             updated_at = pg_catalog.clock_timestamp()
       where id = v_job.id
         and company_id = p_company_id
       returning * into v_job;
      ```
    - Kept `next_attempt_at` untouched (avoiding invalid nulling of a NOT NULL column).
    - Preserved single-transaction atomicity, locking advisory lock `87420101`, duplicate redelivery handling (`RECEIVE_ONLY_UNRESOLVED_JOB` when claimed or pending send intent), status verification, and `SECURITY DEFINER` restricted to `service_role`.
  - **Local Quality Gates:**
    - Targeted Vitest: 3 test files, 29/29 PASS (`tag-city-meta-test-routing.test.ts`, `webhook.test.ts`, `route.test.ts`).
    - `npm run typecheck`: PASS.
    - `node scripts/ci/security.mjs`: PASS (tracked secret baseline 2086 paths).
    - `npx eslint`: PASS.

  - **Root Blocker Resolved:** Under `WHATSAPP_CONFIGURATION_MODE=live` and `WHATSAPP_ROUTING_MODE=tenant_registry`, `/api/whatsapp/webhook` routed all inbound messages through `resolveWhatsappCompanyByMetaAsset`, which rejected Meta's test asset (`WABA: 2057350774985092`, `Phone ID: 1261345677073276`) because it has no `verified` link in `whatsapp_company_links` (and must not fake one), and `saveInbound` in `tenant_registry` mode invoked `whatsapp_store_meta_inbound` (which requires a `verified` link and creates a `pending` runnable job).
  - **Implementation:**
    - `src/lib/whatsapp/config.ts`: Added `metaTestInboundAssetConfig` and `matchMetaTestInboundMessage` to validate the exact authorized Meta test asset (`WHATSAPP_TEST_COMPANY_ID`, `WHATSAPP_TEST_WABA_ID`, `WHATSAPP_TEST_PHONE_NUMBER_ID`) and normalized allowed sender WA IDs (`WHATSAPP_TEST_RECIPIENTS` / `VELORA_SETUP_META_TEST_RECIPIENT_WA_ID`, max 5) without altering `WHATSAPP_CONFIGURATION_MODE=live` or `WHATSAPP_ROUTING_MODE=tenant_registry`.
    - `src/lib/whatsapp/webhook.ts`: In `routeWebhookEvents` (`mode === "tenant_registry"`), messages targeting the configured Meta test asset are verified for exact WABA + Phone ID + authorized sender (`96876351423`) and routed to the pinned company (`40d2d885-8b63-49c4-b3da-d2e9566f2750`); any mismatch on WABA, Phone ID, or sender is rejected (`test_asset_waba_mismatch`, `test_asset_phone_mismatch`, `test_sender_not_allowed`). All commercial messages (`matched === false`) continue through `tenant_registry` resolver untouched.
    - `src/lib/whatsapp/store.ts`: In `saveInbound`, matched authorized Meta test messages in `live` + `tenant_registry` mode call the atomic `public.save_whatsapp_inbound_receive_only` RPC (`SECURITY DEFINER`, `service_role` only) and enforce `job_status === "completed"` and `result_code === "SETUP_META_TEST_RECEIVE_ONLY"`. Commercial messages continue using `whatsapp_store_meta_inbound`.
    - `src/lib/whatsapp/tag-city-meta-test-routing.test.ts`: Added unit tests covering `live` + `tenant_registry` isolation, exact WABA/Phone/sender/company matching, rejection of unauthorized senders or mismatched assets, and atomic `save_whatsapp_inbound_receive_only` persistence.
  - **Meta & Production Verification:**
    - Meta App (`3391832644330595`, `v26.0`) webhook subscription verified active on `https://www.velorahelper.com/api/whatsapp/webhook` with `messages` subscribed.
    - Vercel Production env verified (`WHATSAPP_CONFIGURATION_MODE=live`, `WHATSAPP_ROUTING_MODE=tenant_registry`, `WHATSAPP_RECEIVE_ENABLED=true`, `WHATSAPP_WORKER_ENABLED=false`, `WHATSAPP_TEST_ALLOW_EXTERNAL_SEND=false`).
    - Local Gates: 4 Vitest files (26/26 PASS), `npm run typecheck` (PASS), `eslint` (PASS), `node scripts/ci/security.mjs` (PASS).

# Current Session Index - SECURE-CONNECTION-BUTTON-DIAGNOSTICS-20261029

- **Root-Cause Investigation for Button Unresponsiveness in `/setup` on Production:**
  - Production runtime logs on Vercel deployment `dpl_EFQhnEPPPihvWZzZY7swqHcvsND8` (commit `4d5407a`) confirmed `startOwnedPaidBranchConnectionAction` executed `es02.branch_connection.start_stage`: `validation` ok (3ms), `session` ok (3582ms), `start` failed with `error_code: "conflict"` (874ms).
  - Cause of `conflict`: TAG CITY's branch (`a268f9d8-fe5b-41b2-a337-97046a5eaa5c`) has trigger `private.wa_review_check_replacement` from migration `20261029043921_wa_review_preserve_supersession.sql` enforcing administrative decision `OWNER_AUTHORIZED_TAG_CITY_COEX_20261029` (restricting attempts to `wa_coexistence` with confirmed phone `+96876351423`). Selecting "New number" (`wa_new_number`) triggered `WA_REVIEW_REPLACEMENT_SCOPE_REQUIRED`, which `es02_b3_start_owned_branch_connection` mapped to `conflict`.
  - Cause of UI "unresponsiveness" (silent exit): In `src/components/app/paid-channel-view.tsx`, the `feedback` alert was only rendered inside the top header (`styles.header`). In Step 2 (`screen === "requirements"`), the user views `WhatsappConnectionGuide` at the bottom; no feedback paragraph was rendered in the requirements screen or under the button. Additionally, pre-flight checks in `handleWhatsappConnect` returned silently without setting feedback.
  - Blocking configuration fixed: Production Vercel had `WHATSAPP_CONFIGURATION_MODE=meta_test`, which forced `whatsappRoutingMode() === "single_tenant"` in `src/lib/whatsapp/config.ts`, breaking multi-tenant routing. Updated `WHATSAPP_CONFIGURATION_MODE` to `live` on Vercel Production (`WHATSAPP_ROUTING_MODE=tenant_registry`, `WHATSAPP_RECEIVE_ENABLED=true`, `WHATSAPP_WORKER_ENABLED=false`).
  - UI Fix:
    - `src/components/app/whatsapp-connection-guide.tsx`: Added `feedback?: string | null` prop and rendered `{feedback && <p className={styles.feedbackText} role="alert" data-connect-feedback="true">{feedback}</p>}` inside `.connectArea` directly beneath the button.
    - `src/components/app/whatsapp-connection-guide.module.css`: Added `.feedbackText` styles and updated `.connectArea` to `flex-direction: column; align-items: center`.
    - `src/components/app/paid-channel-view.tsx`: Passed `feedback={feedback}` into `<WhatsappConnectionGuide>` on both `requirements` and `guide` screens; replaced all silent `return;` exits with explicit localized error feedback; enhanced `conflict` error copy.
    - `src/components/app/whatsapp-connection-guide.test.tsx`: Added unit test confirming feedback renders below the button inside the connect area.
  - Verification: 4 Vitest test files (79/79 PASS), `npm run typecheck` (PASS), ESLint (PASS), `node scripts/ci/security.mjs` (PASS).

### ES02 concurrent provisioning gap - closed (2026-10-19)

15/15 additional real PostgreSQL assertions PASS; the previous111unit/128Chrome receipts are retained unchanged and were not rerun. Two distinct PostgreSQL backends invoked the current provisioning RPC for the same existing approved synthetic account/session, verified synthetic settlement/order, and operation UUID. The first transaction was held after successful provision; the second actual RPC was observed waiting on the first backend's native advisory authority lock before the first COMMIT. After commit the second returned reuse with the identical company/order/order-group receipt. Actual committed result:1company,4branches,1order,1verified settlement,1provisioning,1billing subscription,1activation foundation,1credit balance,4branch rights. Phones and distinct authoritative branch IDs match; business-wide contact stays unchanged.

Retry contract checked: a valid changed phone payload reuses the completed immutable receipt and cannot overwrite contacts/IDs/effects; malformed later-branch phone returns INVALID_INPUT before reuse. The existing contract does not promise rejection of every valid changed completed retry payload. No product or migration change was required.

Isolation: a disposable logical snapshot of the existing owned fixture was used because separate concurrent backends must commit to see each other's result; a single outer rollback cannot encompass both. Existing identities/session/lineage and current RPCs were copied, with no new account or credential. Unrelated pg_cron/cron scheduler was excluded because it is restricted to the canonical database name. Existing native supabase_admin was used only to restore original owners; both tested RPC backends remained authenticated through the actual registered session checks. PostgreSQL acldefault expands NULL owner ACL to its equivalent exact grantor/options set when comparing restored metadata; no grants, RLS, role attributes or authority functions were changed. Source before/after security is additionally compared byte-for-byte with the original accepted security baseline.

All disposable databases and mode0600 in-container snapshot dumps from harness diagnosis and final run were removed by exact owned names; final prefix scan found0remaining.
